练习 6ratRel_trans定理附录解答

主定理：ratRel 是传递的。两边各乘一个正分母，把交叉乘积接到同一条链上，再消去多出来的正分母 (n+1)。

theorem ratRel_trans (a b c d e f : Nat) (m n k : Nat)
    (h1 : ratRel
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) m)
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) n))
    (h2 : ratRel
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) n)
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) k)) :
    ratRel
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) m)
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) k) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对 h1、h2 各乘一个正分母（用 mulRep_wd 的两次），再用 mulRep_den_swap 把 (p·dn)·dk 与 (p·dk)·dn 对齐，得到一条从 (p·dk)·dn 到 (r·dm)·dn 的五步链；最后交给 mulRep_den_cancel 消去多出来的 dn。

提示 2目标形态
ratRelxy⇒ratRelyz⇒ratRelxz
提示 3关键件

mulRep_wd（两次）· mulRep_den_swap（三次）· intRel_trans（四次）· mulRep_den_cancel。

查看解答 定理 · 45 行
theorem ratRel_trans (a b c d e f : Nat) (m n k : Nat)
    (h1 : ratRel
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) m)
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) n))
    (h2 : ratRel
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) n)
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) k)) :
    ratRel
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) m)
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) k) :=
  mulRep_den_cancel
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den k))
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den m))
    n
    (intRel_trans
      (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den k)) (den n))
      (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den n)) (den k))
      (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den m)) (den n))
      (mulRep_den_swap a b k n)
      (intRel_trans
        (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den n)) (den k))
        (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den m)) (den k))
        (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den m)) (den n))
        (mulRep_wd
          (Prod.fst Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den n)))
          (Prod.snd Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den n)))
          (Prod.fst Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den m)))
          (Prod.snd Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den m)))
          (Nat.succ k) Nat.zero h1)
        (intRel_trans
          (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den m)) (den k))
          (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k)) (den m))
          (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den m)) (den n))

            (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k)) (den m))
            (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den n)) (den m))
            (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den m)) (den n))
            (mulRep_wd
              (Prod.fst Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k)))
              (Prod.snd Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k)))
              (Prod.fst Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den n)))
              (Prod.snd Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den n)))
              (Nat.succ m) Nat.zero h2)
            (mulRep_den_swap e f n m)))))

有理数相等的传递性

练习 1 · mulRep_den 定理
theorem mulRep_den (p : Prod Nat Nat) (n : Nat) :
    intRel (mulRep p (den n))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Nat.succ n))
                        (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat p) (Nat.succ n))) :=
  congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) =>
      Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Nat.succ n)) x)
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add Nat.zero (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat p) (Nat.succ n)))
      (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat p) (Nat.succ n))
      (zero_add (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat p) (Nat.succ n))))
练习 2 · mulRep_den_mid 定理

先把「两边都乘上同一正分母」的等价假设化成一条 ℕ 加法等式。

theorem mulRep_den_mid (u v : Prod Nat Nat) (n : Nat)
    (h : intRel (mulRep u (den n)) (mulRep v (den n))) :
    Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n))
               (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n)) =
      Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n))
               (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n)) :=
  intRel_trans
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n))
                        (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n)))
    (mulRep u (den n))
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n))
                        (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n)))
    (intRel_symm (mulRep u (den n))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n))
                          (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n)))
      (mulRep_den u n))
    (intRel_trans (mulRep u (den n)) (mulRep v (den n))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n))
                          (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n)))
      h (mulRep_den v n))
练习 3 · mulRep_den_cancel 定理

再证正分母可消去：mulRep u (n+1,0) 与 mulRep v (n+1,0) 差相等时，u 与 v 也差相等。

theorem mulRep_den_cancel (u v : Prod Nat Nat) (n : Nat)
    (h : intRel (mulRep u (den n)) (mulRep v (den n))) :
    intRel u v :=
  mul_cancel_pos n
    (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat u) (Prod.snd Nat Nat v))
    (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Prod.snd Nat Nat u))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat u) (Prod.snd Nat Nat v)) (Nat.succ n))
      (Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n))
               (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n)))
      (myMul (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Prod.snd Nat Nat u)) (Nat.succ n))
      (mul_add_right (Prod.fst Nat Nat u) (Prod.snd Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n))
                 (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n)))
        (Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n))
                 (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n)))
        (myMul (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Prod.snd Nat Nat u)) (Nat.succ n))
        (mulRep_den_mid u v n h)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (myMul (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Prod.snd Nat Nat u)) (Nat.succ n))
          (Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n))
                   (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n)))
          (mul_add_right (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Prod.snd Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n)))))
练习 4 · mulRep_den_den 定理
theorem mulRep_den_den (a b : Nat) :
    intRel (mulRep (den a) (den b))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (Nat.succ a) (Nat.succ b)) Nat.zero) :=
  @Eq.symm.{1} Nat

             (Nat.add Nat.zero (myMul Nat.zero (Nat.succ b))))

      (congrArg.{1} (fun (y : Nat) =>
          Nat.add (myMul (Nat.succ a) (Nat.succ b)) (Nat.add Nat.zero y))
        (zero_mul (Nat.succ b)))
      (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.succ a) (Nat.succ b)) Nat.zero)))

练习 6 · ratRel_trans 定理
theorem ratRel_trans (a b c d e f : Nat) (m n k : Nat)
    (h1 : ratRel
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) m)
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) n))
    (h2 : ratRel
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) n)
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) k)) :
    ratRel
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) m)
      (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) k) :=
  mulRep_den_cancel
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den k))
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den m))
    n
    (intRel_trans
      (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den k)) (den n))
      (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den n)) (den k))
      (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den m)) (den n))
      (mulRep_den_swap a b k n)
      (intRel_trans
        (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den n)) (den k))
        (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den m)) (den k))
        (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den m)) (den n))
        (mulRep_wd
          (Prod.fst Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den n)))
          (Prod.snd Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den n)))
          (Prod.fst Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den m)))
          (Prod.snd Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den m)))
          (Nat.succ k) Nat.zero h1)
        (intRel_trans
          (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den m)) (den k))
          (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k)) (den m))
          (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den m)) (den n))
          (mulRep_den_swap c d m k)
          (intRel_trans
            (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k)) (den m))
            (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den n)) (den m))
            (mulRep (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den m)) (den n))
            (mulRep_wd
              (Prod.fst Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k)))
              (Prod.snd Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (den k)))
              (Prod.fst Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den n)))
              (Prod.snd Nat Nat (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) (den n)))
              (Nat.succ m) Nat.zero h2)
            (mulRep_den_swap e f n m)))))

II.2 · 本章第 7 / 9 单元 · 全书第 26 / 72 单元

26有理数加法的代表元公式与良定义性

Unit 26 — Addition of Rationals

15 个定义 · 45 个定理 · 6 道练习 · 49 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

两个分数相加，分母为什么会变成 (m+1)(n+1)？因为两边的分母用 succ 编码，必须先通分；通分后的分子就是 (a(n+1)+c(m+1)) - (b(n+1)+d(m+1))。把这条公式写成代表元层的 ratAddRep，再证明它对两个因子都良定义——这正是 Quot.lift 需要的两条义务。

分母字段用 succ 编码，所以取 m*n+m+n（㉖.1）：它的 succ 正是公分母 (m+1)(n+1)。整个构造停在整数代表元层：不把加法下降成 Rat → Rat → Rat（那需要函数外延性，本语言没有），也不加任何公理。

def intRel (a b : Prod Nat Nat) : Prop :=
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b) =
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat b) (Prod.snd Nat Nat a)
定义Int声明 2
def Int : Type := @Quot.{1} (Prod Nat Nat) intRel

    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat) => Nat.add a ih) b

def mulRep (a b : Prod Nat Nat) : Prod Nat Nat :=

    (Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b))
             (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat a) (Prod.fst Nat Nat b)))

def ratRep : Type := Prod (Prod Nat Nat) Nat

定义ratRel声明 7
def ratRel (x y : ratRep) : Prop :=
  intRel
    (mulRep (Prod.fst (Prod Nat Nat) Nat x)
            (den (Prod.snd (Prod Nat Nat) Nat y)))
    (mulRep (Prod.fst (Prod Nat Nat) Nat y)
            (den (Prod.snd (Prod Nat Nat) Nat x)))

定理add_shift声明 15
theorem add_shift (a b c d : Nat) :
    Nat.add (Nat.add a c) (Nat.add b d) =
    Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 36 行
  1.   @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add (Nat.add a c) (Nat.add b d))
    (Nat.add a (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))
    (add_assoc a c (Nat.add b d))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add a (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)))
      (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))
      (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat

        (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)) (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d) (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)) (add_assoc c b d))
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)))))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d))

        (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
          (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d) = Nat.add a (Nat.add m d))
          (Nat.add c b) (Nat.add b c)
          (add_comm c b)
          (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d))))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add b c) d))
          (Nat.add a (Nat.add b (Nat.add c d)))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))
          (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
            (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add b c) d) = Nat.add a m)
            (Nat.add (Nat.add b c) d) (Nat.add b (Nat.add c d))
            (add_assoc b c d)
            (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add b c) d))))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
            (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))
            (Nat.add a (Nat.add b (Nat.add c d)))
            (add_assoc a b (Nat.add c d))))))

myMul 在第二个参数上递归，base 就是 0：乘零是定义性相等。

定理myMul_zero声明 16
theorem myMul_zero (a : Nat) :
    myMul a Nat.zero = Nat.zero :=
证明 · 1 步 · 1 行
  1.   Eq.refl Nat.zero

递归步把 myMul a (succ b) 展开成 myAdd a (myMul a b)，也是定义性相等。

定理succ_mul声明 19
theorem succ_mul (a b : Nat) :
    myMul (Nat.succ a) b = Nat.add (myMul a b) b :=
提示 1思路

对 b 归纳。零情形两边都归约成 0；后继情形把左边展开成 (a+1) + myMul (a+1) n，用 ih 把 myMul (a+1) n 换成 a*n + n，再用 succ_add / add_assoc 把两边对到 succ (a + (a*n + n))。

提示 2目标形态
(a+1)×b=a×b+b
提示 3关键件

Nat.rec · Eq.subst（把 ih 换进 add (a+1) 的第二个位置）· succ_add · add_assoc · Eq.refl（add x (succ n) 定义性归约）。

证明 · 1 步 · 25 行
  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => myMul (Nat.succ a) n = Nat.add (myMul a n) n)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ a) Nat.zero) Nat.zero (Nat.add (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero) Nat.zero (add_zero (myMul a Nat.zero))))
    (fun (n : Nat)
         (ih : myMul (Nat.succ a) n = Nat.add (myMul a n) n) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.succ a) m =
        Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
        (Nat.add (myMul a n) n) (myMul (Nat.succ a) n)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ a) n) (Nat.add (myMul a n) n) ih)
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.succ a) (Nat.add (myMul a n) n))
          (Nat.succ (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
          (succ_add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n))
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
            (Nat.succ (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)))
            (Nat.succ (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) n))
            (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
            (congrArg.{1} Nat.succ
              (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) n)
                (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)) (add_assoc a (myMul a n) n)))
            (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) n))))))
    b
定理myMul_comm声明 20
theorem myMul_comm (a b : Nat) :
    myMul a b = myMul b a :=
提示 1思路

对 b 归纳。零情形用 zero_mul 把两边都变成 0；后继情形 myMul a (n+1) = a + myMul a n，用 ih 换成 a + myMul n a，再用 add_comm 换成 myMul n a + a，最后用 succ_mul 的对称收口。

    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero (myMul Nat.zero a)

      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul Nat.zero a) Nat.zero (zero_mul a)))
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : myMul a n = myMul n a) =>
      @Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (myMul (Nat.succ n) a)
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a) (myMul (Nat.succ n) a)
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.add a (myMul n a)) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a)
            (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add a x) ih)
            (add_comm a (myMul n a)))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ n) a) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a) (succ_mul n a))))

㉒.1 加法搬运（分配律步例要用）

㉒.2 左分配律（对 c 归纳）

提示 1思路

㉒.3 右分配律（用交换律化回左分配）

定理add_eq_zero_right声明 30

提示 1思路

    b

定理intRel_trans声明 39
theorem intRel_trans (p q r : Prod Nat Nat)
    (h1 : intRel p q) (h2 : intRel q r) : intRel p r :=
证明 · 1 步 · 21 行
  1.   nat_add_cancel_right
    (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat r))
    (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat r) (Prod.snd Nat Nat p))
    (Prod.snd Nat Nat q)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat r)) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q)) (Prod.snd Nat Nat r))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat r) (Prod.snd Nat Nat p)) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q))
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q)) (Prod.snd Nat Nat r))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat r)) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q))
        (add_rotate (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q) (Prod.snd Nat Nat r)))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q)) (Prod.snd Nat Nat r))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat r) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q)) (Prod.snd Nat Nat p))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat r) (Prod.snd Nat Nat p)) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q))
        (intRel_trans_mid
          (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat p)
          (Prod.fst Nat Nat q) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q)
          (Prod.fst Nat Nat r) (Prod.snd Nat Nat r) h1 h2)
        (add_rotate (Prod.fst Nat Nat r) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q) (Prod.snd Nat Nat p))))

定理mul_shift_c声明 41
theorem mul_shift_c (a b a' b' c : Nat)

定理mulRep_den_mid声明 44
theorem mulRep_den_mid (u v : Prod Nat Nat) (n : Nat)
    (h : intRel (mulRep u (den n)) (mulRep v (den n))) :
    Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n))
               (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n)) =
      Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n))
               (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n)) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 14 行
  1.   intRel_trans
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n))
                        (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n)))
    (mulRep u (den n))
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n))
                        (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n)))
    (intRel_symm (mulRep u (den n))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n))
                          (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n)))
      (mulRep_den u n))
    (intRel_trans (mulRep u (den n)) (mulRep v (den n))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n))
                          (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n)))
      h (mulRep_den v n))

再证正分母可消去：mulRep u (n+1,0) 与 mulRep v (n+1,0) 差相等时，u 与 v 也差相等。

定理intRel_refl声明 45
theorem intRel_refl (p : Prod Nat Nat) : intRel p p :=
提示 1思路

先把 mulRep u (den n) 与 mulRep v (den n) 化到规范形 (u1·d, u2·d)、(v1·d, v2·d)（d = n+1），假设就变成 u1·d + v2·d = v1·d + u2·d；两边分别用 mul_add_right 收成 (u1+v2)·d 与 (v1+u2)·d，最后用 mul_cancel_pos 消去正因子 d。

提示 2目标形态
mulRepu(denn)~mulRepv(denn)⇒u~v
证明 · 1 步 · 1 行
  1.   Eq.refl (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat p))

㉖.1 公分母：succ (m*n + m + n) = (m+1)(n+1)

ratRep 的分母字段用 succ 编码：真正的分母是字段加一。两个有理数相加时公分母是 (m+1)(n+1)，所以结果的分母字段取 m·n+m+n。

定义ratDenRep声明 46
def ratDenRep (m n : Nat) : Nat :=
  Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul m n) m) n

把有理数写成「分子序对 + 分母字段」的规范形状，陈述会好读一些。

定义qrep声明 47
def qrep (a b m : Nat) : ratRep :=
  @prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) m
练习 1ratDenRep_succ定理附录解答
theorem ratDenRep_succ (m n : Nat) :
    Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n) = myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

左边 succ (m*n + m + n) 定义性等于 (m*n + m) + (n+1)；右边用 succ_mul 展开成 m*(n+1) + (n+1)，再用 mul_add 把 m*(n+1) 拆成 m*n + m*1（m*1 定义性归约成 m）。

提示 2目标形态
succ(m×n+m+n)=(m+1)×(n+1)
提示 3关键件

congrArg · mul_add · succ_mul（取对称）· Eq.refl（定义性归约）。

查看解答 定理 · 15 行
theorem ratDenRep_succ (m n : Nat) :
    Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n) = myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))
    (Nat.add (myMul m (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.succ n))
    (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul m (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.succ n))
      (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))
      (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x (Nat.succ n))
        (mul_add m n (Nat.succ Nat.zero))))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
      (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n))
      (Nat.add (myMul m (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.succ n))
      (succ_mul m (Nat.succ n)))

㉖.2 加法的代表元公式

通分后的公式是：a−bm+1 + c−dn+1

=[(a(n+1)+c(m+1))−(b(n+1)+d(m+1))](m+1)(n+1)

分母字段就是 ㉖.1 定义的 ratDenRep m n。

def denOf (x : ratRep) : Nat :=

def ratAddRep (x y : ratRep) : ratRep :=
  @prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat
      (Nat.add
        (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat (numOf x)) (Nat.succ (denOf y)))
        (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat (numOf y)) (Nat.succ (denOf x))))
      (Nat.add
        (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat (numOf x)) (Nat.succ (denOf y)))
        (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat (numOf y)) (Nat.succ (denOf x)))))
    (ratDenRep (denOf x) (denOf y))

㉖.3 从等价假设里取出「交叉和相等」，并消去同一个正分母

theorem ratAdd_cross (a b a' b' : Nat) (m : Nat)
    (h : intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den m))
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (den m))) :
    Nat.add a b' = Nat.add a' b :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

mulRep_den_mid 把假设化成 a·d + b'·d = a'·d + b·d（d = m+1）；两边分别用 mul_add_right 收成 (a+b')·d 与 (a'+b)·d，再用 mul_cancel_pos 消去正因子 d。

提示 2目标形态

(a-b) = (a'-b') 的交叉和形式 a + b' = a' + b。

提示 3关键件

mulRep_den_mid · mul_add_right · mul_cancel_pos · Eq.symm · Eq.trans。

查看解答 定理 · 19 行
theorem ratAdd_cross (a b a' b' : Nat) (m : Nat)
    (h : intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den m))
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (den m))) :
    Nat.add a b' = Nat.add a' b :=
  mul_cancel_pos m (Nat.add a b') (Nat.add a' b)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul (Nat.add a b') (Nat.succ m))
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b' (Nat.succ m)))
      (myMul (Nat.add a' b) (Nat.succ m))
      (mul_add_right a b' (Nat.succ m))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b' (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b (Nat.succ m)))
        (myMul (Nat.add a' b) (Nat.succ m))
        (mulRep_den_mid (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') m h)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (myMul (Nat.add a' b) (Nat.succ m))
          (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b (Nat.succ m)))
          (mul_add_right a' b (Nat.succ m)))))
练习 3ratAdd_num_wd定理附录解答

㉖.4 分子在第一因子上的搬运（两个正分母都乘开）

theorem ratAdd_num_wd (a b a' b' c d m n : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add a b' = Nat.add a' b) :
    Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))) =
      Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

五步链 —— ① add_shift 把四项重排成 (a·n₁ + b'·n₁) + (c·m₁ + d·m₁)；② 两次 mul_add_right 的对称把两个括号收成乘积；③ 用 h 在 n₁ 的因子上改写；④ 再用 mul_add_right 拆开；⑤ add_shift 的对称收回目标配对。

提示 2目标形态
(a·n₁+c·m₁)+(b'·n₁+d·m₁)=(a'·n₁+c·m₁)+(b·n₁+d·m₁)
提示 3关键件

add_shift · mul_add_right（四次，其中两次取对称）· add_congr · congrArg。

查看解答 定理 · 64 行
theorem ratAdd_num_wd (a b a' b' c d m n : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add a b' = Nat.add a' b) :
    Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))) =
      Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
      (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)))
      (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
      (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
    (add_shift (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b' (Nat.succ n))
               (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)))
        (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
      (Nat.add
        (myMul (Nat.add a b') (Nat.succ n))
        (myMul (Nat.add c d) (Nat.succ m)))
      (Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
      (add_congr
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (myMul (Nat.add a b') (Nat.succ n))
          (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)))
          (mul_add_right a b' (Nat.succ n)))
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (myMul (Nat.add c d) (Nat.succ m))
          (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
          (mul_add_right c d (Nat.succ m))))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add a b') (Nat.succ n))
                 (myMul (Nat.add c d) (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add a' b) (Nat.succ n))
                 (myMul (Nat.add c d) (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add
          (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
        (add_congr
          (congrArg.{1} (fun (z : Nat) => myMul z (Nat.succ n)) h)
          (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.add c d) (Nat.succ m))))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add a' b) (Nat.succ n))
                   (myMul (Nat.add c d) (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.add
            (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
            (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
          (Nat.add
            (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
          (add_congr
            (mul_add_right a' b (Nat.succ n))
            (mul_add_right c d (Nat.succ m)))
          (add_shift (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m))
                     (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))))
练习 4ratAdd_num_wd_snd定理附录解答

㉖.5 分子在第二因子上的搬运

theorem ratAdd_num_wd_snd (a b c d c' d' m n : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add c d' = Nat.add c' d) :
    Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m))) =
      Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

与 ㉖.4 同构，只是这次换的是 (c,d) 那一对（乘 m₁ 的项）：add_shift 先把 (c,d') 与 (a,b) 配对，两次 mul_add_right 收成乘积，h 改写 (c+d')，再拆开、add_shift 收回。

提示 2目标形态
(a·n₁+c·m₁)+(b·n₁+d'·m₁)=(a·n₁+c'·m₁)+(b·n₁+d·m₁)
提示 3关键件

add_shift · mul_add_right（四次，其中两次取对称）· add_congr · congrArg。

查看解答 定理 · 64 行
theorem ratAdd_num_wd_snd (a b c d c' d' m n : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add c d' = Nat.add c' d) :
    Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m))) =
      Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
      (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m))))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
      (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m))))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
      (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
    (add_shift (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n))
               (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m)))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
        (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m))))
      (Nat.add
        (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n))
        (myMul (Nat.add c d') (Nat.succ m)))
      (Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
      (add_congr
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n))
          (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
          (mul_add_right a b (Nat.succ n)))
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (myMul (Nat.add c d') (Nat.succ m))
          (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m)))
          (mul_add_right c d' (Nat.succ m))))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n))
                 (myMul (Nat.add c d') (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n))
                 (myMul (Nat.add c' d) (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add
          (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
        (add_congr
          (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n)))
          (congrArg.{1} (fun (z : Nat) => myMul z (Nat.succ m)) h))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n))
                   (myMul (Nat.add c' d) (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.add
            (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
            (Nat.add (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
          (Nat.add
            (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
          (add_congr
            (mul_add_right a b (Nat.succ n))
            (mul_add_right c' d (Nat.succ m)))
          (add_shift (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m))
                     (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))))
练习 5ratAddRep_wd定理附录解答

㉖.6 加法对第一个因子良定义

theorem ratAddRep_wd (a b a' b' c d m n : Nat)
    (h : ratRel (qrep a b m) (qrep a' b' m)) :
    ratRel (ratAddRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n))
           (ratAddRep (qrep a' b' m) (qrep c d n)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

先用 ratAdd_cross 把假设 x ~ x' 化成 a + b' = a' + b，再交给 ㉖.4 得到 A + B' = A' + B。两端分母字段都是同一个 ratDenRep m n，所以直接用 mul_shift_c 得到交叉和等式 A·D + B'·D = A'·D + B·D（D = succ (ratDenRep m n)），它就是 intRel 展开后的样子。用 mulRep_den 把 mulRep 化到规范形，再用 intRel_trans 串起来。

提示 2目标形态
x~x'⇒(x+y)~(x'+y)
提示 3关键件

ratAdd_cross · ratAdd_num_wd · mul_shift_c · mulRep_den（两次）· intRel_trans（两次）· intRel_symm。

查看解答 定理 · 73 行
theorem ratAddRep_wd (a b a' b' c d m n : Nat)
    (h : ratRel (qrep a b m) (qrep a' b' m)) :
    ratRel (ratAddRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n))
           (ratAddRep (qrep a' b' m) (qrep c d n)) :=
  intRel_trans
    (mulRep
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
      (den (ratDenRep m n)))
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat
      (myMul
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
      (myMul
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
    (mulRep
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
      (den (ratDenRep m n)))
    (mulRep_den
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
      (ratDenRep m n))
    (intRel_trans
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (myMul
          (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
        (myMul
          (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (myMul
          (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
        (myMul
          (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
      (mulRep
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat
          (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
        (den (ratDenRep m n)))
      (mul_shift_c
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))
        (ratAdd_num_wd a b a' b' c d m n
          (ratAdd_cross a b a' b' m h)))
      (intRel_symm
        (mulRep
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat
            (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
          (den (ratDenRep m n)))
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat
          (myMul
            (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
          (myMul
            (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
        (mulRep_den
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat
            (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
          (ratDenRep m n))))
练习 6ratAddRep_wd_snd定理附录解答

theorem ratAddRep_wd_snd (a b c d c' d' m n : Nat)

㉖.8 具体数字：1/2 + 1/3 = 5/6

1/2 记成 ((1,0),1)，1/3 记成 ((1,0),2)。按 ㉖.2 的公式：分子 = (1·3 + 1·2) - (0·3 + 0·2) = 5，分母字段 = 1·2 + 1 + 2 = 5（真正分母 6），正是 5/6；它和 10/12 表示同一个有理数。

定理ratAddRep_demo声明 60
theorem ratAddRep_demo :
    ratRel
      (ratAddRep (qrep 1 0 1) (qrep 1 0 2))
      (qrep 10 0 11) :=
提示 1思路

两边都化成代表元后是同一个具体数字；10/12 这一边交叉相乘是 5·12 = 10·6 = 60，全用 Eq.refl 即可（内核把具体数字归约掉）。

提示 2目标形态

(1/2 + 1/3) ~ (10/12)。

提示 3关键件

Eq.refl（具体数字全部归约）。

证明 · 1 步 · 1 行
  1.   Eq.refl (Nat.add (myMul 5 12) (Nat.add Nat.zero (myMul Nat.zero 12)))

单元㉖ 有理数加法的代表元公式与良定义性（ratAddRep）

有理数加法的代表元公式与良定义性

6 道练习 · 6 份按名匹配的解答

练习 1 · ratDenRep_succ 定理
theorem ratDenRep_succ (m n : Nat) :
    Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n) = myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))
    (Nat.add (myMul m (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.succ n))
    (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul m (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.succ n))
      (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))
      (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x (Nat.succ n))
        (mul_add m n (Nat.succ Nat.zero))))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
      (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n))
      (Nat.add (myMul m (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.succ n))
      (succ_mul m (Nat.succ n)))
练习 2 · ratAdd_cross 定理
theorem ratAdd_cross (a b a' b' : Nat) (m : Nat)
    (h : intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den m))
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (den m))) :
    Nat.add a b' = Nat.add a' b :=
  mul_cancel_pos m (Nat.add a b') (Nat.add a' b)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul (Nat.add a b') (Nat.succ m))
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b' (Nat.succ m)))
      (myMul (Nat.add a' b) (Nat.succ m))
      (mul_add_right a b' (Nat.succ m))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b' (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b (Nat.succ m)))
        (myMul (Nat.add a' b) (Nat.succ m))
        (mulRep_den_mid (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') m h)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (myMul (Nat.add a' b) (Nat.succ m))
          (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b (Nat.succ m)))
          (mul_add_right a' b (Nat.succ m)))))
练习 3 · ratAdd_num_wd 定理
theorem ratAdd_num_wd (a b a' b' c d m n : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add a b' = Nat.add a' b) :
    Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))) =
      Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
      (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)))
      (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
      (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
    (add_shift (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b' (Nat.succ n))
               (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)))
        (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
      (Nat.add
        (myMul (Nat.add a b') (Nat.succ n))
        (myMul (Nat.add c d) (Nat.succ m)))
      (Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
      (add_congr
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (myMul (Nat.add a b') (Nat.succ n))
          (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)))
          (mul_add_right a b' (Nat.succ n)))
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (myMul (Nat.add c d) (Nat.succ m))
          (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
          (mul_add_right c d (Nat.succ m))))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add a b') (Nat.succ n))
                 (myMul (Nat.add c d) (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add a' b) (Nat.succ n))
                 (myMul (Nat.add c d) (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add
          (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
        (add_congr
          (congrArg.{1} (fun (z : Nat) => myMul z (Nat.succ n)) h)
          (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.add c d) (Nat.succ m))))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add a' b) (Nat.succ n))
                   (myMul (Nat.add c d) (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.add
            (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
            (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
          (Nat.add
            (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
          (add_congr
            (mul_add_right a' b (Nat.succ n))
            (mul_add_right c d (Nat.succ m)))
          (add_shift (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m))
                     (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))))
练习 4 · ratAdd_num_wd_snd 定理
theorem ratAdd_num_wd_snd (a b c d c' d' m n : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add c d' = Nat.add c' d) :
    Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m))) =
      Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
      (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m))))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
      (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m))))
    (Nat.add
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
      (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
    (add_shift (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n))
               (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m)))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
        (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m))))
      (Nat.add
        (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n))
        (myMul (Nat.add c d') (Nat.succ m)))
      (Nat.add
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
      (add_congr
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n))
          (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
          (mul_add_right a b (Nat.succ n)))
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (myMul (Nat.add c d') (Nat.succ m))
          (Nat.add (myMul c (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m)))
          (mul_add_right c d' (Nat.succ m))))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n))
                 (myMul (Nat.add c d') (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n))
                 (myMul (Nat.add c' d) (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add
          (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
        (add_congr
          (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n)))
          (congrArg.{1} (fun (z : Nat) => myMul z (Nat.succ m)) h))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n))
                   (myMul (Nat.add c' d) (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.add
            (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul b (Nat.succ n)))
            (Nat.add (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
          (Nat.add
            (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
          (add_congr
            (mul_add_right a b (Nat.succ n))
            (mul_add_right c' d (Nat.succ m)))
          (add_shift (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m))
                     (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))))
练习 5 · ratAddRep_wd 定理
theorem ratAddRep_wd (a b a' b' c d m n : Nat)
    (h : ratRel (qrep a b m) (qrep a' b' m)) :
    ratRel (ratAddRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n))
           (ratAddRep (qrep a' b' m) (qrep c d n)) :=
  intRel_trans
    (mulRep
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
      (den (ratDenRep m n)))
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat
      (myMul
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
      (myMul
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
    (mulRep
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
      (den (ratDenRep m n)))
    (mulRep_den
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
      (ratDenRep m n))
    (intRel_trans
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (myMul
          (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
        (myMul
          (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (myMul
          (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
        (myMul
          (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
      (mulRep
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat
          (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
        (den (ratDenRep m n)))
      (mul_shift_c
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))
        (ratAdd_num_wd a b a' b' c d m n
          (ratAdd_cross a b a' b' m h)))
      (intRel_symm
        (mulRep
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat
            (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
          (den (ratDenRep m n)))
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat
          (myMul
            (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
          (myMul
            (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
        (mulRep_den
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat
            (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.add (myMul b' (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
          (ratDenRep m n))))
练习 6 · ratAddRep_wd_snd 定理
theorem ratAddRep_wd_snd (a b c d c' d' m n : Nat)
    (h : ratRel (qrep c d n) (qrep c' d' n)) :
    ratRel (ratAddRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n))
           (ratAddRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c' d' n)) :=
  intRel_trans
    (mulRep
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
      (den (ratDenRep m n)))
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat
      (myMul
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
      (myMul
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
    (mulRep
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m))))
      (den (ratDenRep m n)))
    (mulRep_den
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m))))
      (ratDenRep m n))
    (intRel_trans
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (myMul
          (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
        (myMul
          (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat
        (myMul
          (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
        (myMul
          (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
      (mulRep
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat
          (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
          (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m))))
        (den (ratDenRep m n)))
      (mul_shift_c
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))
        (ratAdd_num_wd_snd a b c d c' d' m n
          (ratAdd_cross c d c' d' n h)))
      (intRel_symm
        (mulRep
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat
            (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m))))
          (den (ratDenRep m n)))
        (@prod_mk Nat Nat
          (myMul
            (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)))
          (myMul
            (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))))
        (mulRep_den
          (@prod_mk Nat Nat
            (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (myMul c' (Nat.succ m)))
            (Nat.add (myMul b (Nat.succ n)) (myMul d' (Nat.succ m))))
          (ratDenRep m n))))

II.2 · 本章第 8 / 9 单元 · 全书第 27 / 72 单元

27有理数乘法的代表元公式与具体计算

Unit 27 — Multiplication of Rationals

23 个定义 · 56 个定理 · 5 道练习 · 40 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

单元⑳ 把 ℚ 建成 Quot ratRel；㉑–㉕ 结清 ratRel 的等价关系；㉖ 定义了加法。现在定义 ℚ 的乘法：a−bm+1 乘 c−dn+1 等于 [(ac+bd) - (ad+bc)] / ((m+1)(n+1))。

分子正是整数代表元的乘法 mulRep；分母字段复用 ㉖.1 的 ratDenRep m n。本单元给出代表元公式、分母公式与交换性等具体计算，为下一单元的乘法良定义性铺路。

全部停在整数代表元层：不把乘法下降成 Rat → Rat → Rat（那需要函数外延性，本语言没有），也不加任何公理。

定义intRel声明 1
def intRel (a b : Prod Nat Nat) : Prop :=
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b) =
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat b) (Prod.snd Nat Nat a)
定义Int声明 2
def Int : Type := @Quot.{1} (Prod Nat Nat) intRel
定义myMul声明 3
def myMul (a b : Nat) : Nat :=
  Nat.rec (fun (_ : Nat) => Nat) Nat.zero
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat) => Nat.add a ih) b
定义mulRep声明 4
def mulRep (a b : Prod Nat Nat) : Prod Nat Nat :=
  @prod_mk Nat Nat
    (Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.fst Nat Nat b))
             (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b)))
    (Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b))
             (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat a) (Prod.fst Nat Nat b)))
定义ratRep声明 5
def ratRep : Type := Prod (Prod Nat Nat) Nat
定义den声明 6
def den (n : Nat) : Prod Nat Nat :=
  @prod_mk Nat Nat (Nat.succ n) Nat.zero
定义ratRel声明 7
def ratRel (x y : ratRep) : Prop :=
  intRel
    (mulRep (Prod.fst (Prod Nat Nat) Nat x)
            (den (Prod.snd (Prod Nat Nat) Nat y)))
    (mulRep (Prod.fst (Prod Nat Nat) Nat y)
            (den (Prod.snd (Prod Nat Nat) Nat x)))
定义Rat声明 8
def Rat : Type := @Quot.{1} ratRep ratRel
定义ratOf声明 9
def ratOf (a : Prod Nat Nat) (n : Nat) : Rat :=
  @Quot.mk.{1} ratRep ratRel
    (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat a n)
定理zero_add声明 10
theorem zero_add (b : Nat) :
    Nat.add Nat.zero b = b :=

    (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)

      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.succ (Nat.add Nat.zero n) = Nat.succ m)
        (Nat.add Nat.zero n) n ih
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add Nat.zero n))))
    b

定理add_shift声明 15
theorem add_shift (a b c d : Nat) :
    Nat.add (Nat.add a c) (Nat.add b d) =
    Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 36 行
  1.   @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add (Nat.add a c) (Nat.add b d))
    (Nat.add a (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))
    (add_assoc a c (Nat.add b d))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add a (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)))
      (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))
      (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add a (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)) = Nat.add a m)
        (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)) (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d) (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)) (add_assoc c b d))
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)))))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d))
        (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add b c) d))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))
        (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
          (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d) = Nat.add a (Nat.add m d))
          (Nat.add c b) (Nat.add b c)
          (add_comm c b)
          (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d))))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add b c) d))
          (Nat.add a (Nat.add b (Nat.add c d)))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))

            (Nat.add (Nat.add b c) d) (Nat.add b (Nat.add c d))

            (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))
            (Nat.add a (Nat.add b (Nat.add c d)))
            (add_assoc a b (Nat.add c d))))))

myMul 在第二个参数上递归，base 就是 0：乘零是定义性相等。

定理succ_mul声明 19
theorem succ_mul (a b : Nat) :
    myMul (Nat.succ a) b = Nat.add (myMul a b) b :=
提示 1思路

对 b 归纳。零情形两边都归约成 0；后继情形把左边展开成 (a+1) + myMul (a+1) n，用 ih 把 myMul (a+1) n 换成 a*n + n，再用 succ_add / add_assoc 把两边对到 succ (a + (a*n + n))。

提示 2目标形态
(a+1)×b=a×b+b
提示 3关键件

Nat.rec · Eq.subst（把 ih 换进 add (a+1) 的第二个位置）· succ_add · add_assoc · Eq.refl（add x (succ n) 定义性归约）。

证明 · 1 步 · 25 行
  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => myMul (Nat.succ a) n = Nat.add (myMul a n) n)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ a) Nat.zero) Nat.zero (Nat.add (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero) Nat.zero (add_zero (myMul a Nat.zero))))
    (fun (n : Nat)
         (ih : myMul (Nat.succ a) n = Nat.add (myMul a n) n) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.succ a) m =
        Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
        (Nat.add (myMul a n) n) (myMul (Nat.succ a) n)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ a) n) (Nat.add (myMul a n) n) ih)
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.succ a) (Nat.add (myMul a n) n))
          (Nat.succ (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
          (succ_add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n))
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
            (Nat.succ (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)))
            (Nat.succ (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) n))
            (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
            (congrArg.{1} Nat.succ
              (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) n)
                (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)) (add_assoc a (myMul a n) n)))
            (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) n))))))
    b
定理myMul_comm声明 20
theorem myMul_comm (a b : Nat) :
    myMul a b = myMul b a :=
提示 1思路

对 b 归纳。零情形用 zero_mul 把两边都变成 0；后继情形 myMul a (n+1) = a + myMul a n，用 ih 换成 a + myMul n a，再用 add_comm 换成 myMul n a + a，最后用 succ_mul 的对称收口。

提示 2目标形态
myMulab=myMulba
提示 3关键件

Nat.rec · congrArg（在 add a 下改写 ih）· add_comm · succ_mul（取对称）。

证明 · 1 步 · 13 行
  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => myMul a n = myMul n a)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero (myMul Nat.zero a)
      (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul Nat.zero a) Nat.zero (zero_mul a)))
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : myMul a n = myMul n a) =>
      @Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (myMul (Nat.succ n) a)
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a) (myMul (Nat.succ n) a)
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.add a (myMul n a)) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a)
            (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add a x) ih)
            (add_comm a (myMul n a)))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ n) a) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a) (succ_mul n a))))
    b

㉒.1 加法搬运（分配律步例要用）

定理add_mid声明 21
theorem add_mid (a x y : Nat) :
    Nat.add a (Nat.add x y) = Nat.add x (Nat.add a y) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 11 行
  1.   @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add a (Nat.add x y))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add a x) y)
    (Nat.add x (Nat.add a y))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add a x) y) (Nat.add a (Nat.add x y)) (add_assoc a x y))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a x) y)
      (Nat.add (Nat.add x a) y)
      (Nat.add x (Nat.add a y))
      (congrArg.{1} (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add m y) (add_comm a x))
      (add_assoc x a y))

㉒.2 左分配律（对 c 归纳）

定理mul_add声明 22
theorem mul_add (a b c : Nat) :
    myMul a (Nat.add b c) = Nat.add (myMul a b) (myMul a c) :=
提示 1思路

对 c 归纳。基例 c = 0 时 b + 0 定义性等于 b、a * 0 定义性等于 0，两边都是 a*b，Eq.refl 即可；步例把 a * (b + succ n) 展开成 a + a*(b+n)，用 ih 换成 a + (a*b + a*n)，再交给 add_mid 换成 a*b + (a + a*n)。

提示 2目标形态
myMula(b+c)=myMulab+myMulac
提示 3关键件

Nat.rec · congrArg（在 add a 下改写 ih）· add_mid · Eq.refl（定义性展开）。

证明 · 1 步 · 16 行
  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => myMul a (Nat.add b n) = Nat.add (myMul a b) (myMul a n))
    (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (myMul a b))
    (fun (n : Nat)
         (ih : myMul a (Nat.add b n) = Nat.add (myMul a b) (myMul a n)) =>
      @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (myMul a (Nat.add b (Nat.succ n)))
        (Nat.add a (myMul a (Nat.add b n)))
        (Nat.add (myMul a b) (myMul a (Nat.succ n)))
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a (Nat.add b n))))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add a (myMul a (Nat.add b n)))
          (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a b) (myMul a n)))
          (Nat.add (myMul a b) (myMul a (Nat.succ n)))
          (congrArg.{1} (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add a m) ih)
          (add_mid a (myMul a b) (myMul a n))))
    c

㉒.3 右分配律（用交换律化回左分配）

定理mul_add_right声明 23
theorem mul_add_right (a b c : Nat) :
    myMul (Nat.add a b) c = Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b c) :=
提示 1思路

把左边用 myMul_comm 翻成 c * (a+b)，交给 mul_add 展开，再用两次 myMul_comm（一次对 c a，一次对 c b）把因子顺序摆回来。

提示 2目标形态
myMul(a+b)c=myMulac+myMulbc
提示 3关键件

myMul_comm · mul_add · congrArg（两次，分别改第一个与第二个加数）· Eq.trans。

证明 · 1 步 · 16 行
  1.   @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (myMul (Nat.add a b) c)
    (myMul c (Nat.add a b))
    (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b c))
    (myMul_comm (Nat.add a b) c)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul c (Nat.add a b))
      (Nat.add (myMul c a) (myMul c b))
      (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b c))
      (mul_add c a b)
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul c a) (myMul c b))
        (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul c b))
        (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b c))
        (congrArg.{1} (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add m (myMul c b)) (myMul_comm c a))
        (congrArg.{1} (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add (myMul a c) m) (myMul_comm c b))))

㉒.4 结合律（对 c 归纳，步例用左分配）

    c

㉓.1 两个加数同时改写

㉔.1 加法的后继展开（消去律的脚手架）

| zero => Nat.zero
| succ m => m

定理add_eq_zero_right声明 30

提示 1思路

    b
定理mul_eq_zero_right声明 31

定理mul_succ_cancel声明 32

再证正分母可消去：mulRep u (n+1,0) 与 mulRep v (n+1,0) 差相等时，u 与 v 也差相等。

定理intRel_refl声明 45
theorem intRel_refl (p : Prod Nat Nat) : intRel p p :=
提示 1思路

先把 mulRep u (den n) 与 mulRep v (den n) 化到规范形 (u1·d, u2·d)、(v1·d, v2·d)（d = n+1），假设就变成 u1·d + v2·d = v1·d + u2·d；两边分别用 mul_add_right 收成 (u1+v2)·d 与 (v1+u2)·d，最后用 mul_cancel_pos 消去正因子 d。

提示 2目标形态
mulRepu(denn)~mulRepv(denn)⇒u~v
证明 · 1 步 · 1 行
  1.   Eq.refl (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat p))

㉖.1 公分母：succ (m*n + m + n) = (m+1)(n+1)

ratRep 的分母字段是「succ 编码」：真正的分母 = 字段 + 1。两个有理数相加时公分母是 (m+1)(n+1)，所以结果的分母字段取 m*n + m + n。

定义ratDenRep声明 46
def ratDenRep (m n : Nat) : Nat :=
  Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul m n) m) n

把有理数写成「分子序对 + 分母字段」的规范形状，陈述会好读一些。

定义qrep声明 47
def qrep (a b m : Nat) : ratRep :=
  @prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) m
定理ratDenRep_succ声明 48
theorem ratDenRep_succ (m n : Nat) :
    Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n) = myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n) :=
提示 1思路

左边 succ (m*n + m + n) 定义性等于 (m*n + m) + (n+1)；右边用 succ_mul 展开成 m*(n+1) + (n+1)，再用 mul_add 把 m*(n+1) 拆成 m*n + m*1（m*1 定义性归约成 m）。

提示 2目标形态
succ(m×n+m+n)=(m+1)×(n+1)
提示 3关键件

congrArg · mul_add · succ_mul（取对称）· Eq.refl（定义性归约）。

证明 · 1 步 · 13 行
  1.   @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))
    (Nat.add (myMul m (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.succ n))
    (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul m (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.succ n))
      (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))
      (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x (Nat.succ n))
        (mul_add m n (Nat.succ Nat.zero))))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
      (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n))
      (Nat.add (myMul m (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.succ n))
      (succ_mul m (Nat.succ n)))

㉖.2 加法的代表元公式

a−bm+1 + c−dn+1

=[(a(n+1)+c(m+1))−(b(n+1)+d(m+1))](m+1)(n+1)

分母字段就是 ㉖.1 的 ratDenRep m n。

def denOf (x : ratRep) : Nat :=

㉓.2 第一个分量：两边各自展开，再在规范形上换配对

先把左边 ((a·c + b·d)·e + (a·d + b·c)·f) 展开成四个乘积。

右边 a·(c·e + d·f) + b·(c·f + d·e) 同样展开成四个乘积。

再把左边四个乘积用结合律改成规范形。

规范形上换一次配对：add_shift 把中间两个加数对调，再用 add_comm 把第四个与第二个加数对调。

㉓.5 拼成 intRel：fst(left) + snd(right) = fst(right) + snd(left)

具体例子：(2,1)·(3,1) 得 (7,5)，再乘 (4,1)；与先乘 (3,1)·(4,1) 的结果代表元相等。

            (add_shift (myMul a' c) (myMul b c) (myMul b' d) (myMul a d)))
          (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
            (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d)) (Nat.add (myMul b c) (myMul a d)) =
              Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d)) m)
            (Nat.add (myMul b c) (myMul a d))
            (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
            (add_comm (myMul b c) (myMul a d))
            (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d)) (Nat.add (myMul b c) (myMul a d))))))))
定理mulRep_wd_snd声明 72
theorem mulRep_wd_snd (a b a' b' c d : Nat)
    (h : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b')) :
    intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
           (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b')) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 16 行
  1.   @Eq.subst.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
    (fun (y : Prod Nat Nat) =>
      intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b)) y)
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b'))
    (mulRep_comm a' b' c d)
    (@Eq.subst.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
      (fun (x : Prod Nat Nat) =>
        intRel x (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)))
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
      (@Eq.symm.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
        (mulRep_comm c d a b))
      (mulRep_wd a b a' b' c d h))

具体例子：2-0 与 3-1 差相等，都用 4-1 乘在前面，结果仍差相等——(4,1)*(2,0) = (8,2)、(4,1)*(3,1) = (13,7)，而 8+7 = 13+2 = 15。

先把「分母的整数代表元乘一个正整数」化成规范形：mulRep p (n+1,0) 与 (p1·(n+1), p2·(n+1)) 差相等。

㉗.1 乘法的代表元公式

a−bm+1 * c−dn+1

=[(ac+bd)−(ad+bc)](m+1)(n+1)

分子正是整数代表元的乘法 mulRep；分母字段仍用 ㉖.1 的 ratDenRep m n。

定义ratMulRep声明 73
def ratMulRep (x y : ratRep) : ratRep :=
  @prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat
    (mulRep (numOf x) (numOf y))
    (ratDenRep (denOf x) (denOf y))
提示 1思路

mulRep p (n+1,0) 的定义性展开是 (p1·(n+1) + p2·0, p1·0 + p2·(n+1))；把 p2·0 与 p1·0 归约成 0，再用 zero_add 把右边第二分量的 0 + p2·(n+1) 搬到 p2·(n+1)。

提示 2目标形态

mulRep p (den n) ~ (p1·(n+1), p2·(n+1))。

提示 3关键件

congrArg · Eq.symm · zero_add（乘零由定义性归约）。

      qrep
        (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d))
        (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
        (ratDenRep m n) :=

  1.   Eq.refl (qrep
    (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d))
    (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
    (ratDenRep m n))

㉗.2 具体数字

1/2 * 1/3：分子 (1·1 + 0·0, 1·0 + 0·1) = (1,0)，分母字段 1·2+1+2 = 5（真正分母 6），正是 1/6。

theorem ratMul_demo_one :
    ratRel
      (ratMulRep (qrep 1 0 1) (qrep 1 0 2))
      (qrep 1 0 5) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

两边化到代表元后是同一个具体数字，全部由内核归约，Eq.refl 即可。

提示 2目标形态

(1/2 * 1/3) ~ 1/6。

提示 3关键件

Eq.refl。

II.2 · 本章第 9 / 9 单元 · 全书第 28 / 72 单元

28有理数乘法的良定义性

Unit 28 — Multiplication is Well-Defined

23 个定义 · 59 个定理 · 2 道练习 · 55 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

单元⑳ 把 ℚ 建成 Quot ratRel；㉑–㉕ 结清 ratRel；㉖ 定义加法；㉗ 给出乘法的代表元公式：a−bm+1 乘 c−dn+1 = [(ac+bd) - (ad+bc)] / ((m+1)(n+1))。

现在把乘法在代表元层结清：固定一个因子，另一个因子换成 ratRel 等价但不同的代表元时，乘积仍等价。两条义务是 ratMulRep_wd（x ~ x' 推出 x·y ~ x'·y）与 ratMulRep_wd_snd（y ~ y' 推出 x·y ~ x·y'）。

两条都化归到整数层已经证过的 mulRep_wd 与 mulRep_wd_snd：先用 ratAdd_cross（同分母的 ratRel 可消去正分母）把假设化成整数代表元的相等，再让整数乘法把这份相等搬到两个分子上，最后乘回公分母。仍然停在整数代表元层：不把乘法下降成 Rat → Rat → Rat（那需要函数外延性，本语言没有），也不加任何公理。

定义intRel声明 1
def intRel (a b : Prod Nat Nat) : Prop :=
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b) =
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat b) (Prod.snd Nat Nat a)
定义Int声明 2
def Int : Type := @Quot.{1} (Prod Nat Nat) intRel
定义myMul声明 3
def myMul (a b : Nat) : Nat :=
  Nat.rec (fun (_ : Nat) => Nat) Nat.zero
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat) => Nat.add a ih) b
定义mulRep声明 4
def mulRep (a b : Prod Nat Nat) : Prod Nat Nat :=
  @prod_mk Nat Nat
    (Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.fst Nat Nat b))
             (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b)))
    (Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b))
             (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat a) (Prod.fst Nat Nat b)))
定义ratRep声明 5
def ratRep : Type := Prod (Prod Nat Nat) Nat
定义den声明 6
def den (n : Nat) : Prod Nat Nat :=
  @prod_mk Nat Nat (Nat.succ n) Nat.zero
定义ratRel声明 7
def ratRel (x y : ratRep) : Prop :=
  intRel
    (mulRep (Prod.fst (Prod Nat Nat) Nat x)
            (den (Prod.snd (Prod Nat Nat) Nat y)))
    (mulRep (Prod.fst (Prod Nat Nat) Nat y)
            (den (Prod.snd (Prod Nat Nat) Nat x)))
定义Rat声明 8
def Rat : Type := @Quot.{1} ratRep ratRel
定义ratOf声明 9
def ratOf (a : Prod Nat Nat) (n : Nat) : Rat :=
  @Quot.mk.{1} ratRep ratRel
    (@prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat a n)

theorem add_shift (a b c d : Nat) :
    Nat.add (Nat.add a c) (Nat.add b d) =

定理succ_mul声明 19
theorem succ_mul (a b : Nat) :
    myMul (Nat.succ a) b = Nat.add (myMul a b) b :=
提示 1思路

对 b 归纳。零情形两边都归约成 0；后继情形把左边展开成 (a+1) + myMul (a+1) n，用 ih 把 myMul (a+1) n 换成 a*n + n，再用 succ_add / add_assoc 把两边对到 succ (a + (a*n + n))。

提示 2目标形态
(a+1)×b=a×b+b
提示 3关键件

Nat.rec · Eq.subst（把 ih 换进 add (a+1) 的第二个位置）· succ_add · add_assoc · Eq.refl（add x (succ n) 定义性归约）。

证明 · 1 步 · 25 行

    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ a) Nat.zero) Nat.zero (Nat.add (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero) Nat.zero (add_zero (myMul a Nat.zero))))
    (fun (n : Nat)
         (ih : myMul (Nat.succ a) n = Nat.add (myMul a n) n) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.succ a) m =
        Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
        (Nat.add (myMul a n) n) (myMul (Nat.succ a) n)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ a) n) (Nat.add (myMul a n) n) ih)
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.succ a) (Nat.add (myMul a n) n))
          (Nat.succ (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
          (succ_add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n))

                (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)) (add_assoc a (myMul a n) n)))
            (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) n))))))
    b
定理myMul_comm声明 20
theorem myMul_comm (a b : Nat) :
    myMul a b = myMul b a :=
提示 1思路

对 b 归纳。零情形用 zero_mul 把两边都变成 0；后继情形 myMul a (n+1) = a + myMul a n，用 ih 换成 a + myMul n a，再用 add_comm 换成 myMul n a + a，最后用 succ_mul 的对称收口。

提示 2目标形态
myMulab=myMulba
提示 3关键件

Nat.rec · congrArg（在 add a 下改写 ih）· add_comm · succ_mul（取对称）。

证明 · 1 步 · 13 行
  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => myMul a n = myMul n a)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero (myMul Nat.zero a)
      (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul Nat.zero a) Nat.zero (zero_mul a)))
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : myMul a n = myMul n a) =>
      @Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (myMul (Nat.succ n) a)
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a) (myMul (Nat.succ n) a)
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.add a (myMul n a)) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a)
            (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add a x) ih)
            (add_comm a (myMul n a)))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ n) a) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a) (succ_mul n a))))
    b

㉒.1 加法搬运（分配律步例要用）

㉒.2 左分配律（对 c 归纳）

定理mul_add声明 22
theorem mul_add (a b c : Nat) :
    myMul a (Nat.add b c) = Nat.add (myMul a b) (myMul a c) :=
提示 1思路

对 c 归纳。基例 c = 0 时 b + 0 定义性等于 b、a * 0 定义性等于 0，两边都是 a*b，Eq.refl 即可；步例把 a * (b + succ n) 展开成 a + a*(b+n)，用 ih 换成 a + (a*b + a*n)，再交给 add_mid 换成 a*b + (a + a*n)。

提示 2目标形态
myMula(b+c)=myMulab+myMulac
提示 3关键件

Nat.rec · congrArg（在 add a 下改写 ih）· add_mid · Eq.refl（定义性展开）。

证明 · 1 步 · 16 行
  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => myMul a (Nat.add b n) = Nat.add (myMul a b) (myMul a n))
    (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (myMul a b))
    (fun (n : Nat)
         (ih : myMul a (Nat.add b n) = Nat.add (myMul a b) (myMul a n)) =>
      @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (myMul a (Nat.add b (Nat.succ n)))
        (Nat.add a (myMul a (Nat.add b n)))
        (Nat.add (myMul a b) (myMul a (Nat.succ n)))
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a (Nat.add b n))))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add a (myMul a (Nat.add b n)))
          (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a b) (myMul a n)))
          (Nat.add (myMul a b) (myMul a (Nat.succ n)))
          (congrArg.{1} (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add a m) ih)
          (add_mid a (myMul a b) (myMul a n))))
    c

㉒.3 右分配律（用交换律化回左分配）

定理mul_add_right声明 23

提示 1思路

㉒.4 结合律（对 c 归纳，步例用左分配）

㉔.1 加法的后继展开（消去律的脚手架）

前驱 pred (succ n) = n 是定义性相等：用它把前驱等式换回原等式。

def Nat.pred (n : Nat) : Nat := match n with
| zero => Nat.zero
| succ m => m

本语言没有内建的 Nat.succ.inj：对等式两边取前驱即得（定义性相等）。

定理add_eq_zero_right声明 30

提示 1思路

    b

    b
定理mul_succ_cancel声明 32

    myMul m (Nat.succ n) = myMul d (Nat.succ n) :=

      Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Nat.succ n)) x)

      (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat p) (Nat.succ n))
      (zero_add (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat p) (Nat.succ n))))

先把「两边都乘上同一正分母」的等价假设化成一条 ℕ 加法等式。

定理mulRep_den_mid声明 44
theorem mulRep_den_mid (u v : Prod Nat Nat) (n : Nat)
    (h : intRel (mulRep u (den n)) (mulRep v (den n))) :
    Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n))
               (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n)) =
      Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n))
               (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n)) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 14 行
  1.   intRel_trans
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n))
                        (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n)))
    (mulRep u (den n))
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n))
                        (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n)))
    (intRel_symm (mulRep u (den n))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n))
                          (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat u) (Nat.succ n)))
      (mulRep_den u n))
    (intRel_trans (mulRep u (den n)) (mulRep v (den n))
      (@prod_mk Nat Nat (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n))
                          (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat v) (Nat.succ n)))
      h (mulRep_den v n))

再证正分母可消去：mulRep u (n+1,0) 与 mulRep v (n+1,0) 差相等时，u 与 v 也差相等。

定理intRel_refl声明 45
theorem intRel_refl (p : Prod Nat Nat) : intRel p p :=
提示 1思路

先把 mulRep u (den n) 与 mulRep v (den n) 化到规范形 (u1·d, u2·d)、(v1·d, v2·d)（d = n+1），假设就变成 u1·d + v2·d = v1·d + u2·d；两边分别用 mul_add_right 收成 (u1+v2)·d 与 (v1+u2)·d，最后用 mul_cancel_pos 消去正因子 d。

提示 2目标形态
mulRepu(denn)~mulRepv(denn)⇒u~v
证明 · 1 步 · 1 行
  1.   Eq.refl (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat p))

㉖.1 公分母：succ (m*n + m + n) = (m+1)(n+1)

ratRep 的分母字段是「succ 编码」：真正的分母 = 字段 + 1。两个有理数相加时公分母是 (m+1)(n+1)，所以结果的分母字段取 m*n + m + n。

定义ratDenRep声明 46
def ratDenRep (m n : Nat) : Nat :=
  Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul m n) m) n

把有理数写成「分子序对 + 分母字段」的规范形状，陈述会好读一些。

定义qrep声明 47
def qrep (a b m : Nat) : ratRep :=
  @prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) m
定理ratDenRep_succ声明 48
theorem ratDenRep_succ (m n : Nat) :
    Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n) = myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n) :=
提示 1思路

左边 succ (m*n + m + n) 定义性等于 (m*n + m) + (n+1)；右边用 succ_mul 展开成 m*(n+1) + (n+1)，再用 mul_add 把 m*(n+1) 拆成 m*n + m*1（m*1 定义性归约成 m）。

提示 2目标形态
succ(m×n+m+n)=(m+1)×(n+1)
提示 3关键件

congrArg · mul_add · succ_mul（取对称）· Eq.refl（定义性归约）。

证明 · 1 步 · 13 行
  1.   @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))
    (Nat.add (myMul m (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.succ n))
    (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul m (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.succ n))
      (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n))
      (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x (Nat.succ n))
        (mul_add m n (Nat.succ Nat.zero))))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
      (myMul (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n))
      (Nat.add (myMul m (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.succ n))
      (succ_mul m (Nat.succ n)))

㉖.2 加法的代表元公式

a−bm+1 + c−dn+1

=[(a(n+1)+c(m+1))−(b(n+1)+d(m+1))](m+1)(n+1)

分母字段就是 ㉖.1 的 ratDenRep m n。

㉓.2 第一个分量：两边各自展开，再在规范形上换配对

具体例子：(2,1)·(3,1) 得 (7,5)，再乘 (4,1)；与先乘 (3,1)·(4,1) 的结果代表元相等。

定理mulRep_comm声明 70
theorem mulRep_comm (a b c d : Nat) :

      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c))
                 (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d)))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c))
                 (Nat.add (myMul b' d) (myMul a d)))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d))
                 (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)))
        (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
          (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c)) (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d)) =
            Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c)) m)
          (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d))
          (Nat.add (myMul b' d) (myMul a d))
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
            (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d))
            (Nat.add (myMul a' d) (myMul b d))
            (Nat.add (myMul b' d) (myMul a d))
            (add_comm (myMul b d) (myMul a' d))
            (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
              (Nat.add (myMul a' d) (myMul b d))
              (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b' d))
              (Nat.add (myMul b' d) (myMul a d))
              (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
                (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b' d))
                (Nat.add (myMul a' d) (myMul b d))
                (mul_shift_c a b a' b' d h))
              (add_comm (myMul a d) (myMul b' d))))
          (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c)) (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d)))))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c))
                   (Nat.add (myMul b' d) (myMul a d)))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d))
                   (Nat.add (myMul b c) (myMul a d)))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d))
                   (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
            (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d))
                     (Nat.add (myMul b c) (myMul a d)))
            (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c))
                     (Nat.add (myMul b' d) (myMul a d)))
            (add_shift (myMul a' c) (myMul b c) (myMul b' d) (myMul a d)))
          (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
            (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d)) (Nat.add (myMul b c) (myMul a d)) =
              Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d)) m)
            (Nat.add (myMul b c) (myMul a d))
            (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
            (add_comm (myMul b c) (myMul a d))
            (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d)) (Nat.add (myMul b c) (myMul a d))))))))
定理mulRep_wd_snd声明 72
theorem mulRep_wd_snd (a b a' b' c d : Nat)
    (h : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b')) :
    intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
           (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b')) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 16 行
  1.   @Eq.subst.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
    (fun (y : Prod Nat Nat) =>
      intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b)) y)
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b'))
    (mulRep_comm a' b' c d)
    (@Eq.subst.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
      (fun (x : Prod Nat Nat) =>
        intRel x (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)))
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
      (@Eq.symm.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
        (mulRep_comm c d a b))
      (mulRep_wd a b a' b' c d h))

具体例子：2-0 与 3-1 差相等，都用 4-1 乘在前面，结果仍差相等——(4,1)*(2,0) = (8,2)、(4,1)*(3,1) = (13,7)，而 8+7 = 13+2 = 15。

先把「分母的整数代表元乘一个正整数」化成规范形：mulRep p (n+1,0) 与 (p1·(n+1), p2·(n+1)) 差相等。

㉗.1 乘法的代表元公式

a−bm+1 * c−dn+1

=[(ac+bd)−(ad+bc)](m+1)(n+1)

分子正是整数代表元的乘法 mulRep；分母字段仍用 ㉖.1 的 ratDenRep m n。

定义ratMulRep声明 73
def ratMulRep (x y : ratRep) : ratRep :=
  @prod_mk (Prod Nat Nat) Nat
    (mulRep (numOf x) (numOf y))
    (ratDenRep (denOf x) (denOf y))
提示 1思路

mulRep p (n+1,0) 的定义性展开是 (p1·(n+1) + p2·0, p1·0 + p2·(n+1))；把 p2·0 与 p1·0 归约成 0，再用 zero_add 把右边第二分量的 0 + p2·(n+1) 搬到 p2·(n+1)。

提示 2目标形态

mulRep p (den n) ~ (p1·(n+1), p2·(n+1))。

提示 3关键件

congrArg · Eq.symm · zero_add（乘零由定义性归约）。

定理ratMulRep_mk声明 74
theorem ratMulRep_mk (a b c d m n : Nat) :
    ratMulRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n) =
      qrep
        (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d))
        (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
        (ratDenRep m n) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 4 行
  1.   Eq.refl (qrep
    (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d))
    (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
    (ratDenRep m n))
定理ratMul_den声明 75
theorem ratMul_den (x y : ratRep) :
    denOf (ratMulRep x y) =
    ratDenRep (denOf x) (denOf y) :=
提示 1思路

ratMulRep 的分母字段就是 ratDenRep (denOf x) (denOf y)，定义性相等。

提示 2目标形态
denOf(x×y)=ratDenRep(denOfx)(denOfy)
提示 3关键件

Eq.refl（定义性归约）。

证明 · 1 步 · 1 行
  1.   Eq.refl (ratDenRep (denOf x) (denOf y))
定理ratMul_num_comm声明 76
theorem ratMul_num_comm (a b c d : Nat) :
    mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) =
      mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) :=
提示 1思路

两个整数代表元相乘可以交换，直接引用整数层的 mulRep_comm。

提示 2目标形态
mulRep(a,b)(c,d)=mulRep(c,d)(a,b)
提示 3关键件

mulRep_comm。

证明 · 1 步 · 1 行
  1.   mulRep_comm a b c d

㉗.2 具体数字

1/2 * 1/3：分子 (1·1 + 0·0, 1·0 + 0·1) = (1,0)，分母字段 1·2+1+2 = 5（真正分母 6），正是 1/6。

定理ratMul_demo_one声明 77
theorem ratMul_demo_one :
    ratRel
      (ratMulRep (qrep 1 0 1) (qrep 1 0 2))
      (qrep 1 0 5) :=
提示 1思路

两边化到代表元后是同一个具体数字，全部由内核归约，Eq.refl 即可。

提示 2目标形态

(1/2 * 1/3) ~ 1/6。

提示 3关键件

Eq.refl。

证明 · 1 步 · 1 行
  1.   @Eq.refl.{1} Nat 6

1/2 * 2/3 = 2/6 = 1/3：左边分子 (2,0)、分母字段 5；交叉相乘 2·3 = 1·6。

2/3 * 3/4 = 6/12 = 1/2：分子 6、分母字段 11。

同分母的 ratRel 可以消去正分母：(a-b)/(m+1) ~ (a'-b')/(m+1) 就是 a + b' = a' + b。乘法两条义务都要先把假设化成这个形状。

定理rat_cross声明 80
theorem rat_cross (a b a' b' : Nat) (m : Nat)
    (h : intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (den m))
                (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (den m))) :
    Nat.add a b' = Nat.add a' b :=
证明 · 1 步 · 15 行
  1.   mul_cancel_pos m (Nat.add a b') (Nat.add a' b)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul (Nat.add a b') (Nat.succ m))
      (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b' (Nat.succ m)))
      (myMul (Nat.add a' b) (Nat.succ m))
      (mul_add_right a b' (Nat.succ m))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b' (Nat.succ m)))
        (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b (Nat.succ m)))
        (myMul (Nat.add a' b) (Nat.succ m))
        (mulRep_den_mid (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') m h)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (myMul (Nat.add a' b) (Nat.succ m))
          (Nat.add (myMul a' (Nat.succ m)) (myMul b (Nat.succ m)))
          (mul_add_right a' b (Nat.succ m)))))
练习 1ratMulRep_wd定理附录解答

㉘.1 乘法对第一个因子良定义

theorem ratMulRep_wd (a b a' b' c d m n : Nat)
    (h : ratRel (qrep a b m) (qrep a' b' m)) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n))
           (ratMulRep (qrep a' b' m) (qrep c d n)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

ratAdd_cross 先把 x ~ x' 化成整数代表元的相等 a + b' = a' + b；mulRep_wd 把它搬到两个乘积分子上；最后再用一次 mulRep_wd，把两个分子同时乘上公分母 (m+1)(n+1)，正好就是 ratRel 的定义。

提示 2目标形态

x ~ x' ⇒ x*y ~ x'*y（同分母 m、同一个 y）。

提示 3关键件

ratAdd_cross · mulRep_wd（两次，第二次的乘数是 den (ratDenRep m n)）。

查看解答 定理 · 11 行
theorem ratMulRep_wd (a b a' b' c d m n : Nat)
    (h : ratRel (qrep a b m) (qrep a' b' m)) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n))
           (ratMulRep (qrep a' b' m) (qrep c d n)) :=
  mulRep_wd
    (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d))
    (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
    (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d))
    (Nat.add (myMul a' d) (myMul b' c))
    (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)) Nat.zero
    (mulRep_wd a b a' b' c d (rat_cross a b a' b' m h))
练习 2ratMulRep_wd_snd定理附录解答

㉘.2 乘法对第二个因子良定义

theorem ratMulRep_wd_snd (a b c d c' d' m n : Nat)
    (h : ratRel (qrep c d n) (qrep c' d' n)) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n))
           (ratMulRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c' d' n)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

与 ㉘.1 同构，只是这次把 ratAdd_cross 用在 y ~ y' 上；整数层的 mulRep_wd_snd 把 c + d' = c' + d 搬到 x 的两个乘积分子上，再乘回公分母。

提示 2目标形态

y ~ y' ⇒ x*y ~ x*y'（同分母 n、同一个 x）。

提示 3关键件

ratAdd_cross（作用在 y 上）· mulRep_wd_snd · mulRep_wd。

查看解答 定理 · 11 行
theorem ratMulRep_wd_snd (a b c d c' d' m n : Nat)
    (h : ratRel (qrep c d n) (qrep c' d' n)) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n))
           (ratMulRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c' d' n)) :=
  mulRep_wd
    (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d))
    (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
    (Nat.add (myMul a c') (myMul b d'))
    (Nat.add (myMul a d') (myMul b c'))
    (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)) Nat.zero
    (mulRep_wd_snd c d c' d' a b (rat_cross c d c' d' n h))

练习 1 · ratMulRep_wd 定理
theorem ratMulRep_wd (a b a' b' c d m n : Nat)
    (h : ratRel (qrep a b m) (qrep a' b' m)) :
    ratRel (ratMulRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n))
           (ratMulRep (qrep a' b' m) (qrep c d n)) :=
  mulRep_wd
    (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d))
    (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
    (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d))
    (Nat.add (myMul a' d) (myMul b' c))
    (Nat.succ (ratDenRep m n)) Nat.zero
    (mulRep_wd a b a' b' c d (rat_cross a b a' b' m h))

    ratRel (ratMulRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c d n))
           (ratMulRep (qrep a b m) (qrep c' d' n)) :=

    (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d))
    (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
    (Nat.add (myMul a c') (myMul b d'))
    (Nat.add (myMul a d') (myMul b c'))

    (mulRep_wd_snd c d c' d' a b (rat_cross c d c' d' n h))
